# పాత్రల రిఫరెన్స్ ఇమేజ్‌లు (Character Reference Sheets)

Google Flow లో పాత్రలు ప్రతి క్లిప్‌లో ఒకేలా కనిపించడానికి, ముందుగా ప్రతి పాత్రకు **ఒక్కసారి** ఇమేజ్ తయారు చేయండి.

1. కింది ప్రాంప్ట్‌తో Flow (లేదా ఏదైనా ఇమేజ్ టూల్) లో ఇమేజ్ తయారు చేయండి. 3–4 వెర్షన్లలో ఉత్తమమైనది ఎంచుకోండి.
2. దాన్ని `Valmiki_Ramayanam/_reference_images/` ఫోల్డర్‌లో ఇచ్చిన పేరుతో సేవ్ చేయండి (ఉదా: `RAMA_PRINCE.png`).
3. ప్రతి క్లిప్‌లో **రిఫరెన్స్** అని ఉన్న ఇమేజ్‌లను Flow లో *Ingredients* గా జత చేసి, ఆ క్లిప్ ప్రాంప్ట్ ఇవ్వండి.
4. ఒకసారి ఆమోదించిన ఇమేజ్‌ను మార్చవద్దు. మారిస్తే ఆ పాత్ర ఉన్న అన్ని క్లిప్‌లు మళ్ళీ చేయాలి.

పాత్రలు, గుంపులు, వస్తువులు, ప్రదేశాలు – క్లిప్‌ల్లో **రిఫరెన్స్** లో ఉన్న ప్రతిదానికీ ఒక ఇమేజ్.

## `IDEAL_MAN.png` · ఆదర్శ పురుషుడు – వాల్మీకి ఊహలోని 16 గుణాల వ్యక్తి (నీలం కాదు)

```text
Character reference image for animation consistency: one character only, full body, standing, facing the camera, centred, plain soft warm-grey background, even soft lighting, calm expression, vertical 9:16. Very high quality: ultra-detailed, sharp and clean 4K-quality render, rich colour depth, film-quality lighting, no blur, no noise, no artefacts. Style: traditional Indian devotional epic art brought to life as premium cinematic 3D animation, inspired by classical Indian temple murals and traditional devotional paintings of Hindu gods, stylized (not photorealistic) characters with divine, graceful, dignified features faithful to traditional Hindu iconography, every character with a clearly individual face (different face shape, eyes, nose, age and build) so that no two characters ever share the same face, rich jewel-tone colours with temple-gold accents, ancient South Indian temple-era architecture; forest and ashram settings are lush, green and serene with flowering trees, clear rivers and streams, deer, peacocks and birds (darker only where the scene says so); volumetric god rays, soft glowing divine light, high detail, smooth fluid motion, reverent devotional mood. Character: The Ideal Man, an imagined figure of the perfect human being that Valmiki pictures while asking his question (not yet revealed as Rama): noble young man about twenty-five, warm golden-wheatish human skin and never blue, tall graceful build, gentle serene smile, long dark hair tied in a simple half-knot, always clean-shaven, plain white cotton dhoti and angavastram with a thin gold border, no crown and no jewellery except a simple rudraksha on a thread, a bow only when the scene shows one, a soft golden glow outlining him with a faint dreamlike shimmer at the edges to show he is imagined, a small sandalwood tilak on the forehead, distinctive face: oval face with a gently rounded chin, large lotus-petal-shaped eyes, long straight nose, full calm lips, broad forehead, softly arched brows, warm golden-wheatish human skin. This is a sacred Hindu devotional scene from the Valmiki Ramayana: depict every deity and character with deep reverence and faithful traditional Indian iconography. Everyone is fully and modestly dressed in traditional Indian attire (women in properly draped silk or cotton sarees covering the shoulders and body, men in dhoti with angavastram or bark-cloth); no revealing, Western, fantasy-armour or modern clothing; no sexualised or suggestive poses; no blood, wounds, gore or graphic violence; no horror or jump-scare look; no comedy, cartoon exaggeration or caricature; no distorted, mocking or disrespectful depiction of gods; no modern objects, buildings, vehicles or technology. No text, letters, logos or watermarks.
```
